function onPageShowEvents() {

} else {
initCartEvent();
}
function initCartEvent() {
window.fetch(window.theme.routes.cart + '.js').then((response) => {
if (!response.ok) {
throw {status: response.statusText};
}
return response.json();
}).then((response) => {
document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme:cart:change', {
detail : {
cart: response,
},
bubbles: true,
}));
return response;
}).catch((e) => {
console.error(e);
});
}
};

// Mega-menu category titles: keep each on a single line by shrinking the
// font (via --fit-scale) only when the text would otherwise overflow.
// At 16px a title like "7-OH ALTERNATIVES" fits down to ~420px wide;
// anything narrower (or any longer label) is scaled down to fit.
(function () {
if (window.__navMegaFitTitles) return;
window.__navMegaFitTitles = true;
var MIN_SCALE = 0.6; // never shrink below ~9.6px
function fit(title) {
var text = title.querySelector('.navmega__cat-title__text');
if (!text) return;
// Measure at full size.
text.style.setProperty('--fit-scale', '1');
var cs = getComputedStyle(title);
var avail = title.clientWidth
- parseFloat(cs.paddingLeft || 0)
- parseFloat(cs.paddingRight || 0);
var full = text.scrollWidth;
if (avail <= 0 || full <= 0) return; // hidden — leave for when shown
var scale = avail / full;
if (scale >= 1) { scale = 1; }
else if (scale < MIN_SCALE) { scale = MIN_SCALE; }
text.style.setProperty('--fit-scale', String(scale));
}
function init() {
var titles = document.querySelectorAll('[data-fit-title]');
if (!titles.length) return;
if ('ResizeObserver' in window) {
// Fires on viewport resize AND when a title goes from hidden
// (0 width) to visible — covers desktop dropdown + mobile drawer.
var ro = new ResizeObserver(function (entries) {
for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) fit(entries[i].target);
});
titles.forEach(function (t) { ro.observe(t); });
} else {
var run = function () { titles.forEach(fit); };
run();
window.addEventListener('resize', run);
}
}
if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
} else {
init();
}
})();

};

primary_language: document.documentElement.lang,
};
const blockId = "ARXAxdldzdDJ3ZmgvM__judge_me_reviews_testimonials_carousel_3KWXH6";
function loadSpecificScript() {
const scriptSrc = shared.carousel_type === 'videos' || shared.carousel_type === 'cards' ? 'https://cdn.shopify.com/extensions/01a043e7-c58c-7d4a-944e-492e0b18ec3b/judgeme-725/assets/media_carousel.js' : 'https://cdn.shopify.com/extensions/01a043e7-c58c-7d4a-944e-492e0b18ec3b/judgeme-725/assets/testimonials_carousel.js';
if (document.querySelector(`script[src="${scriptSrc}"]`)) { initializeCarousel(); return; }
const script = document.createElement('script');
script.src = scriptSrc;
script.onload = function() { setTimeout(initializeCarousel, 50); };
script.onerror = function() { console.error('Error loading script:'); setTimeout(initializeCarousel, 100); };
document.head.appendChild(script);
}
function initializeCarousel() {
if (!window.jdgmCarouselUtils || !window.jdgmCarouselMode) { setTimeout(initializeCarousel, 50); return; }
const buildFunction = shared.carousel_type === 'videos' || shared.carousel_type === 'cards' ? window.jdgmBuildMediaCards : window.jdgmBuildTestimonialCards;
if (buildFunction) {
const start = function() { window.jdgmCarouselUtils.initCarousel(blockId, settings, averageRating, params, buildFunction); };
start();
} else {
setTimeout(initializeCarousel, 100);
}
}
loadSpecificScript();
})();

WARNING: Kratom and its derivative products are addictive if not used as directed. Misuse or overuse significantly increases the risk of dependence. Do not exceed suggested serving amounts. Do not use more than twice a week. Do not combine with alcohol, medications, or other substances. May cause impairment. Do not drive or operate machinery after use. Not for individuals under 21, or those who are pregnant or nursing. Keep out of reach of children. Please read our
disclaimer
.

What’s the difference between kava and kratom?
<div class="accordion__body no-js-accordion rte body-size-4"
data-accordion-body
id="accordion-text_iQKiqd">
Kava is traditionally used for gentle social relaxation. Kratom may offer subtle energy at low servings or deeper calm at higher ones. Read more about
kava vs kratom
and find out how these two botanical-based ingredients can complement each other to support you everyday wellness.

What is 7 OH?
<div class="accordion__body no-js-accordion rte body-size-4"
data-accordion-body
id="accordion-text_QC48HQ">
7 OH (
otherwise referred to as 7-OH, 7-hydroxy, and 7-hydroxymitragynine
) is a potent kratom alkaloid that occurs naturally in the kratom plant (
Mitragyna speciosa
). Through clean concentration techniques, we’re able to pinpoint and extract this potent alkaloid to create a powerful product that’s designed to naturally boost your every day.

best 7-hydroxymitragynine dose

//age verification pop up
$0(document).ready(function() {
//?noagegate=1 bypasses the pop up and marks the visitor as verified for later navigations
var ageGateOverride = false;
try {
ageGateOverride = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('noagegate') === '1';
} catch (e) {
}
if (ageGateOverride) {
localStorage.setItem('freshAgeVerification', 'shown');
}
//if the user has not already verified their age, display a age verification pop up.
//keep this delay short: the scroll lock below is applied at the same moment the pop up
//fades in, so a long delay lets the visitor scroll away before the gate ever appears.
if (localStorage.getItem('freshAgeVerification') != 'shown') {
$0('#freshAgeVerificationPopUp').delay(500).fadeIn(200);
$0('body').addClass('popup-no-scroll');
}
//close the pop up and mark in local storage so the pop up does not display again
$0('#freshAgeVerificationPopUpClose').click(function(e) {
e.preventDefault();
localStorage.setItem('freshAgeVerification', 'shown');
$0('#freshAgeVerificationPopUp').fadeOut();
$0('body').removeClass('popup-no-scroll');
});
//leave the page
$0('#freshAgeVerificationPopUpBack').click(function(e) {
e.preventDefault();
window.location.href = 'https://www.google.com/';
});
});
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
const originalUpdate = window.updatePriceAndSellingPlanInfo;
if (typeof originalUpdate === 'function') {
window.updatePriceAndSellingPlanInfo = function(...args) {
try {
originalUpdate.apply(this, args);
} catch (e) {
if (
e.message.includes('querySelector') &&
e.message.includes('null')
) {
//console.warn("⚠️ Selling plan element nije pronađen (verovatno nedostaje u DOM-u):", e.message);
} else {
throw e;
}
}
};
}
});
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
const loopInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[id*=\'_one_time_purchase\']');
const loopBlocks = document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\'subscription-i18n\'], .shopify_subscriptions_app_block_label, .shopify_subscriptions_app_block_container, .subscriptions_app_embed_block');
loopInputs.forEach(input => input.remove());
loopBlocks.forEach(el => el.remove());
});
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
const target = document.querySelector('[data-left-to-spend]');
const FREE_SHIPPING_THRESHOLD = 75;
if (!target) return;
const forceUpdate = () => {
if (target.innerText.trim() !== `$${FREE_SHIPPING_THRESHOLD.toFixed(2)}`) {
target.innerText = `$${FREE_SHIPPING_THRESHOLD.toFixed(2)}`;
}
};
forceUpdate();
const observer = new MutationObserver(forceUpdate);
observer.observe(target, {childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true});
});

function b64DecodeUnicode(str) {
try {
return decodeURIComponent(
atob(str)
.split('')
.map(function (c) {
return '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2);
})
.join(''),
);
} catch {
return str;
}
}
